# Agents · S · part 21

897 entries, self-issuer-mcp to senior-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **self-issuer-mcp**: `mcp: { authorizationServer: { server, authorization, resource?, scopes? } }` makes the app its own issuer: `runtime.mcpSelfIssuer` feeds `createHostedMcpResourceAuth({ runtime })`, which verifies tokens in process (`server.verifyAccessToken(token, resource)`) and calls `validateGrant` on every request. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/app-runtime/agents/self-issuer-mcp.md) · ★7)
- **self-journal**: Iterative improvements to agent behavior, process, and conventions on this project. **Not** a substitute for [EXPERIMENT_LOG.md](../research/EXPERIMENT_LOG.md) or [DISCUSSION_NOTES.md](../research/DISCUSSION_NOTES.md) — those hold research findings; this holds **how we work better**. ([cpuguy96/StepCOVNet](https://github.com/cpuguy96/StepCOVNet/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/self-journal.md) · ★22)
- **self-leanness-auditor**: Read-only auditor for the Leanness lens of /self-audit — flags machinery in the skills, the skills/_lib/ helpers, validate.sh, the driver and agents/code-reviewer.md that is correct today but does not earn its place — a branch, option or function nothing reaches, a thin wrapper with one caller, a guard for a state the callers rule out, a rule restated in full where a pointer to its single owner would do, a mode that adds branches but no capability, and state or output nobody reads. ([SpaiR/task-pipeline](https://github.com/SpaiR/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/self-leanness-auditor.md) · ★7)

- **self-managed-prs**: The goal: an agent takes a well-specified issue to a merged PR with a human involved only where judgement is needed. This document defines how much autonomy is granted, how work moves through labels, and the guardrails that keep it safe. Autonomy is **granted per repository, per level, in a PR to this file**; the default is L1. ([edwinjosechittilappilly/shipshape-stack](https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/shipshape-stack/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/self-managed-prs.md))

- **self-org-coach**: Use this subagent for heavyweight, context-isolated Holacracy work that would otherwise flood the main conversation — consuming a full Governance Meeting transcript, an org-wide policy/role audit across every circle, a deep tension-triage pass over a circle's backlog, or cascade-proposal drafting. - **self-organization**: Designs and maintains feedback loops that let the knowledge graph modify its own structure safely. Co sesję fabryki/co tydzień generuje `knowledge-base/self-pilot-reports/&lt;date&gt;.md` z 3 sekcji (version-proposals dla 33 agentów library + pilot-orchestrator audyt 3 top agentów + mistake-recorder audyt errors-*.md). - **self-reflect**: Reads the just-completed unit of work and emits a structured good/bad self-reflection list — each finding names a concrete moment and carries one route {learnings | ticket | none} with a one-line justification. Assembles and yields to the parent (/reflect); issues no AskUserQuestion and performs no project-side write. Invoked by /reflect. Does not write code. ([maratik123/lab-game](https://github.com/maratik123/lab-game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/self-reflect.md) · 2 copies)
- **self-reflect**: Validates proposed PM actions against rules before file writes execute. Checks that estimates come from the user, task completions are confirmed, scope changes are consented to, and actuals are computed from timestamps. Returns VALID or INVALID with reason. ([rvanbaalen/skills](https://github.com/rvanbaalen/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/pm/agents/self-reflect.md))
- **Self-Reflection**: Two-pass quality scoring on agent outputs. Scores findings 0-10, re-ranks, filters low-confidence items. Inspired by PR-Agent's self-reflection pattern. ([Doumajnik/template](https://github.com/Doumajnik/template/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/self-reflection.agent.md) · ★3)
- **self-reflection**: Use whenever an ObjectCore gate/eval failure is reported, described, or observed — the user says `bun run check`/`bun run eval` is red, names a failing check (activation, output, coverage, readiness, or delegation), or pastes a failure — to diagnose the root cause and capture any durable lesson in the knowledge base. Delegate as soon as a failure is named or shown, not only after running the gate yourself. ([smithdak/objectcore](https://github.com/smithdak/objectcore/blob/HEAD/plugins/reflection/agents/self-reflection.md))
- **self-reflection**: 自己改善ループの META 振り返りエージェント。detect_acceptance_signal hook が「ok / 完了」等の acceptance シグナルで spawn する (user からは直接起動しない)。直前セッションをまず『user と Claude の対話』として一次レンズで読み解き (user の framing に Claude が収束したか乖離したか)、次に効率・プロセス改善を二次軸として発掘し、**障害の瞬間に届く層へ落とす**（到達性で常時層 / hook / memory を選ぶ — memory が既定ではない）。判断と起案のみ — settings/CLAUDE.md は直接編集せず PROPOSAL ブロックで親へ返す（in-session 決着・永続キュー不使用）。user には話しかけない (結果は親が 1 行で出す)。 ([YutoMizutani/pantheon](https://github.com/YutoMizutani/pantheon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/self-reflection.md))
- **self-reflection-auditor-v3**: Phase 4.5 quality gate — pre-PR self-review. Asks "If I were reviewing this PR, what would I flag?" Use proactively when: Phase 4.5 reached, pre-PR review needed, implementation complete. Auto-triggers: self-review, self-reflection, pre-PR, phase 4.5, quality gate ([stelitsyn/lacrimosa](https://github.com/stelitsyn/lacrimosa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/self-reflection-auditor-v3.md) · ★31)

- **self-review**: Independent reviewers spend most of their rounds on the same few defect classes. Each one is cheaper to catch while you still have the code open than after a publish, a review and a repair round. Apply this list to your own diff after `npm run verify` and before you commit. ([alondero/buildmesh](https://github.com/alondero/buildmesh/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/self-review.md) · ★5)
- **self-review**: Reflexion and validation partner for any work product — plans, designs, brainstorm outputs, implementations. Use when the user explicitly asks for an independent second pass on a finished work product (/sc:review, "review this plan before I share it"). Not for run-and-report test evidence — verifier; not for a security audit — /sc:analyze --focus security. ([ajitta/superclaude](https://github.com/ajitta/superclaude/blob/HEAD/src/superclaude/agents/self-review.md) · ★3)
- **self-review**: 你是 Fenix Daily Self-Review Agent（每日自省）——负责每日工作回顾（add-daily-self-review）。 ([fenixsoft/fenixd](https://github.com/fenixsoft/fenixd/blob/HEAD/agents/self-review.md) · ★1)
- **self-review**: Reviews implementation diff against spec and design with a maximally skeptical mindset and issues APPROVE / REJECT. Invoked by /task after Verify (Step 10) and reused by /project-review, /bugfix, and any post-push fix round to validate post-fix state. ([JeriC4o/agent-harness](https://github.com/JeriC4o/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/self-review.md))
- **self-review**: Reviews implementation diff against spec and design with a maximally skeptical mindset and issues APPROVE / REJECT. Invoked by /task after Verify (Step 10) and reused by /project-review to validate the post-fix state. ([maratik123/lab-game](https://github.com/maratik123/lab-game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/self-review.md) · 3 copies)
- **self-review**: Find every sentence in a diff that has no source in the brief. ([talentsia/talentsia-skills](https://github.com/talentsia/talentsia-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/talentsia-engineering/skills/implement-from-a-brief/agents/self-review.md))
- **self-review**: Reviews a pull request diff for quality, security, and consistency before submission. Produces actionable findings categorized by severity. ([ubiquitousthey/exo](https://github.com/ubiquitousthey/exo/blob/HEAD/agents/self-review.md))
- **self-review-applier**: The writing hand of the self-review loop. Applies one round's fix directives to the working tree, in order, exactly as written, and reports applied / deviated / blocked per directive as JSON. Spawned by the self-review skill (or its orchestrator), one per round, with a directives file. Has no shell — it cannot run tests or git; the caller re-runs pre-flight. ([HoussemDjeghri/self-review](https://github.com/HoussemDjeghri/self-review/blob/HEAD/agents/self-review-applier.md) · ★1)

- **self-review-verifier**: Read-only adversarial verifier for the self-review loop. Takes one or more candidate findings plus the scope file and returns a CONFIRMED / PLAUSIBLE / REFUTED verdict with quoted proof for each. Never edits files. ([HoussemDjeghri/self-review](https://github.com/HoussemDjeghri/self-review/blob/HEAD/agents/self-review-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **self-review-worker**: General-purpose worker with Swival self-review. Use for implementation, file edits, generated artifacts, and other tasks where you want Swival's `--self-review` loop to take a second pass before accepting output. ([sheurich/agent-skills](https://github.com/sheurich/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/packages/swival-subagent/agents/self-review-worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **self-reviewer**: Reviews own session changes for quality gaps, missing tests, and simplification opportunities. Dispatched in phase 4b (Refine) before external review. Outputs structured JSON findings per findings-schema.md. ([redpanda-data/ui-harness](https://github.com/redpanda-data/ui-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/self-reviewer.md) · ★34 · 2 copies)
- **self-reviewer**: Reviews the AI Autonomous agent's own change before it becomes a pull request; runs in CI on a different model than the implementer. ([Netcracker/qubership-apihub](https://github.com/Netcracker/qubership-apihub/blob/HEAD/tools/agent/opencode/agents/self-reviewer.md) · ★17)
- **self-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer of GOVERNANCE TEXT — agent definitions, the oversight contract, governance docs, and the rules encoded in framework scripts. Reviews rules, not programs, across seven categories (contradiction, governance-hole, unenforceable, loop, gaming, stale-status, ownership). Invoked as a one-shot subprocess by scripts/framework/validate_self.sh, which supplies the corpus and the output schema. Does NOT review application code, diffs, or implementation correctness — those belong to code-reviewer and the other inner-loop reviewers.
